# AWS Badge Library

> Canonical Shields.io badge library for AWS services.
>
> Service names are sourced from [`aws-services.md`](../services/cloud/aws-services.md). Badge links use the official AWS Documentation search because the inventory intentionally stores service names, not per-service documentation URLs.
